## Calls page: for Kim, where each call is and who has paid (Nov 12)

- (Carlo, Nov 12, 8:09 p.m.: "on the calls page it's still confusing; it's for Kim and all she needs to know is what calls were done, what are being done, what will be done in the future, and who has paid and who has not. Being able to sort by type or tech is nice but not that necessary".) **Where the Calls notes above say steps 1 to 4, Show the money in the top row, and the Still to do / Done / Cancelled boxes, read this.** Nothing about which calls are loaded or how money is counted changed. `renderCallsShell`, `runCalls` (its drawing is now the inner `draw()`, so a click on a box does not load the calls again).
- **Top**: "Kim, pick the days. Then click a box to see only those calls." with Start over and Help. **1 Which days?** is as before.
- **2 Where is each call?** five boxes with counts (`.clk-row`, `button.cl-stat.clk[data-cview]`, `CALLS.view`): **Every call** · **✅ Done** · **🔧 Being done now** (on the way or on site) · **📅 Still to come** (booked, no day yet, needs another visit) · **✕ Cancelled** (only when there are some). **3 Who has paid?** three boxes that count only the calls that are done (`.clk-pay`): **💰 Done and paid** (every invoice of the call has nothing owing) · **❗ Done, not paid yet** (an invoice still owes) · **📝 Done, no invoice yet**. A click shows only those; a second click goes back to every call. The counts are of the days picked (the calls are always loaded with every status now; `CALLS.status` is not used).
- **The list's last two columns are "Where is it?" and "Paid?"** in plain coloured words (`.clk-w-*`, `.clk-p-*`): Done / Being done now: on site / Still to come / Needs another visit / Cancelled, and ✓ Paid / Not paid: owes $226.00 / No invoice yet / Not done yet, with the invoice number (a link) and its total under it. Copy list copies the calls showing, with a Paid column.
- **"More: one kind of call, one tech, or the money totals"** is one closed line under Which days? (`details#cl-kmore`): What kind of call?, Which tech? and 💲 Show the money totals are inside. It opens by itself when one of them is in use. Start over closes it and clears them.
- **The Calls page is in the app's theme** (Carlo, Nov 12, 8:15 p.m.: "the same colour and square buttons as the other pages we have recently changed"): the eight count boxes are dark blue squares (up to 190px wide, 128px high, the name on top and the number big under it; **Done and paid is green**; the picked one has the yellow frame); the day buttons, the day arrows, the kind-of-call chips and the tech button are dark blue with white words (the picked one has the yellow frame; the old coloured chips and glow are gone); the Which days? panel is white with a thin edge (it was dark grey); the list is white with pale blue day bands. One CSS block right before the copy buttons' block, all under `html body[data-page="calls"] #main article.calls.cl-clean` with `!important`, because this page has many older strong styles. Looked at in a test picture. Not seen on the live app, where a person's own page colours (Page settings) may still tint the page behind it. **Softened at 8:17 p.m.** ("soften up the look of all buttons"): every button and box on the page (the count boxes, the day buttons and arrows, kind of call, the tech button, Start over and Help) is the soft look now: white to pale blue, a thin pale blue edge, dark blue words, 12px corners; the picked one is filled pale blue with a dark blue edge (no yellow frame); Done and paid is pale green; the number on Done, not paid yet is red. A second CSS block right after the first. Looked at in a test picture.
- **The Calls page's own sand and night-teal theme is gone** (Carlo, Nov 12, 8:25 p.m., with pictures of Calls, tan with a teal top bar, beside Supplier invoices, white with a black top bar: "I told you to make the colours match the other pages; they do not match; why are you not doing what I am asking"). The Calls page had a whole CSS block of its own, "SAND THEME (whole Calls page)", from an older colour picture of his: tan page (`--sd-page` #f1e3cc), night-teal top bar, sand-coloured day bands and money box. I had only restyled the buttons and missed that the page itself was a different colour. The block (53 lines) was taken out, so the page and its top bar now use the app's own colours like every other page; the money box and Copy list got the app's white and soft look. **Lesson: when he says match the colours of the other pages, look at the whole page beside another page (background and top bar first), not only the buttons. Check a page for its own theme block before restyling pieces of it.** Looked at in a test picture (no tan left). Not seen on the live app.
- **✕ Cancel this call on each line** (Carlo, Nov 12, 8:18 p.m.: "have a button that lets me cancel the call if I know it's a mistake"). The last column of the list, on every call that is not done and not already cancelled (`.clk-cxbtn`, `data-cl-cancel` = the visit's id; owners and people with `calls.dispatch` or `calls.book`). It asks "Cancel this call?" with the customer, day, time, kind and job (Yes, cancel this call / No, keep it), then `set_call_status(visit, 'cancelled', 'Cancelled from the Calls page: booked by mistake')`: the call leaves the dispatch board, a job note is written, and the line reads Cancelled without loading the page again. It is cancel, not delete: it can be put back from the dispatch board. A call that came from ServiceTitan may come back at the next sync if it is still booked there (not checked). Checked: the script parses. **The click was not run and it was not seen on the live app.**
- **The name opens the customer, the job opens the invoice or the job, and each call has its Attached buttons** (Carlo, Nov 12, 8:20 p.m.: "if I click on the name have it open the customer file, but if I click on the job section have it open the job or invoice; also attach any document or picture you can to it as a short cut"). In the list: the customer's name is underlined like a link and opens the customer's page (a click on the time, address, tech or the other plain parts of the line still does too). **The Job box** (`td.clk-jobcell`, `data-cl-job`) opens the call's invoice (`openInvoice`, the first invoice that is not void); a call with no invoice yet opens the customer's page, scrolled to that job in Job history with it opened (`openJob`). The job's summary (what the call is for, 140 letters) shows under the job number. **A new column, Attached** (`td.cust-att.clk-att[data-att-jobs]`, filled by `attFillJobs` → `job_attachments`, the same as the customer's page): 📄 supplier invoices and 📦 POs (people who see costs), the job's files (signed invoices, PDFs, videos), 📷 photos, 🧾 remittances (owners); a click opens it in a box over the page. The hint line and ❓ Help say it. Checked: the script parses. **Not run, not looked at in a picture and not seen on the live app; the list is wider now.**
- **The five day choices in 1 Which days? are small equal squares** (Carlo, Nov 12, 8:34 p.m.: "make section one small square boxes and remember make the text fit… don't make it look sloppy or messy, you do that often"): Today, Past week, Past month, Next week, Next month are 86px squares, one word on each line (`button[data-preset]`, `.cl-pt { width: min-content }`), lined up with the "Or pick one day" box. Also, the names on the count boxes may wrap to two lines ("Done, no invoice yet" touched the edge of its box). Measured in a test browser: all five 86 by 86 with their words inside; looked at in a picture. **Not seen on the live app. Lesson, again: measure that words sit inside their box and look at the picture before sending.**
- **Attached on each call now has everything** (Carlo, Nov 12, 8:35 p.m.: "attach all documents and pictures to the files that load"). The Attached column first shows the call's own records in green: 🧾 its invoices (from the call's row) and 📝 its proposals (`job_attachments` now also answers `e`, the job's proposals: written into `sync/extra/258_job_attachments.sql`, live Nov 12; drawn only where the box has `data-att-est`, so the customer's page does not show them twice), then as before 📄 supplier invoices, 📦 POs, the job's files, 📷 photos, 🧾 remittances. A call with nothing says "Nothing attached" (`data-att-none`). I took "the files that load" to mean the calls in the list. Checked: the script parses; the function answers on the live database. **Not seen on the live app.**
- **The Calls tab is named "Search Calls"** (Carlo, Nov 12, 9:06 p.m.: "change the tab name of calls to Search Calls"): the side bar button (`#calls-link`), the page's name in the top bar and on Back buttons (`PAGE_NAMES.calls`), and Find a page answers to "search calls". Tech Calls (the techs' own page) is not changed. In these notes "the Calls page" is this page. Checked: the script parses. **Not seen on the live app.**
- ❓ Help has the new steps. Checked: the script parses; the real page code was run in a test browser with 14 pretend calls: the boxes count right, Done not paid yet showed its 1 call, a second click brought back all 14; looked at in a picture. **Not seen on the live app. "Paid" goes by the invoices tied to the call's job; a job with several visits shows the same invoices on each visit.**

## Partners page: made fast for the Operations manager (Nov 10)

- (Carlo, Nov 10, 8:53 p.m.: "my Operations manager uses that, he is smart but very busy so I am looking to speed him up as much as possible"; I listed ten ideas; "do it all".) **For him: fewer clicks, no first question screen, nothing that hides what he needs.** Nothing about the money or how it is counted changed. No database change: it all reads `partner_stores`, `partner_cards` and `partner_ledger` (waiting) for each partner.
- **"Mike, 6 partner invoices need you today."** A dark blue framed box at the top of the Partners first page (`ppNeedHtml`, `PPN`, `ppNeedLoad`, `ppnFetch`, `ppnKind`, `ppnGroups`, `ppnLine`, `ppnWire`): four boxes with counts and dollars, **✉ Can not be sent yet** (a we-bill-them partner's invoice with something missing: signature, account #, approval #; first, oldest first) · **⏰ Should be paid by now** (older than that partner's own `terms_days`, 30 when it has none; most late first) · **✏ Need the % and the fee** (`pay.needs_hand`) · **📋 Open on the booking lists** (one line a partner, from `partner_cards.open`). Click a box to see its lines (8, then "Show all"). Each line: partner, customer, invoice, what it needs, amount, and **Open it ›**, which opens that partner on Payments → Waiting with the invoice number already in its Find box (`ppGo`), so the line to type in is right there. He did not say which job is first, so I chose this order.
- **🔎 Find in every partner** (in the same box, `#ppn-q`): customer, invoice number, the partner's own number, street. It looks through the invoices waiting for payment in every partner; **Look in the paid ones too** loads the rest once (`PPN.paid`).
- **Each card says what is waiting**: a red "3 need you" tag, cards with the most first; a card with nothing is greyed with "✓ Nothing waiting on you" (`ppNeedBy`, `.ppl-need`, `.ppl-quiet`).
- **↩ Back to Costco, where you were** button at the top (the last partner he opened, `PP.store`; its tab and view were already remembered). I did not make the page jump straight into the last partner, because then he would not see what needs him.
- **A number on the Partners button in the side bar** (`ppBadge`): invoices that need someone (not the leads). Looked up 25 seconds after the app opens and every 15 minutes, for people who can do partner pay.
- **Opens at once**: the first page draws from what it had last time (`localStorage reid.ppl`: the partner totals and counts) and the newest comes in behind (`ppListDraw`; `openPartners` only fetches).
- **On a partner's own page** (`renderPartners`): waiting invoices are **oldest first**; each has "42 days · 12 late" under its date and a **yellow / amber / red strip** at 1+, 31+, 61+ days past the partner's terms (`ppLateCls`, `.pp-late1..3`); **✓ Pick all that are ready (n)** beside Pick all shown (`#pp-ready`, `ppIsReady`: nothing missing, no % to type, money still owed), then the usual Record a payment / Mark settled bar; **Enter in a box of the list saves it and jumps to the same box on the next line**, and the page is not drawn again under him while he is typing in the list: only the Ready / Needs tags are freshened, the rest catches up when he leaves the list (`PP.dirty`, the guard at the top of `renderPartners`, `ppReadyHtml`, `data-pp-rdy`).
- **⚙ Settings holds the rarely used things**. First page (`#ppl-set`, `PP.lset`): **it opens as one box at the top of the page, right under the blue heading** (Carlo, Nov 10, 9:00 p.m.: "the setting button should open it at the top not the bottom"; `.ppl-setbox`): Add a partner, Switched off, then one line for each partner with its "Use for sales" tick and its logo links (a picture can be dropped on the line). Nothing of it is on the cards or at the bottom any more. Partner page (`#pp-set`, class `pp-set-on`, `PP.pset`): Add a partner / Edit, Switched off, the partner's rules, its usual terms. It opens by itself while adding or editing, and when invoices are still on the old % cut (the Convert button lives there). Dropping a logo on a card still works with it closed.
- Checked: the script parses; the first page was run in a test browser with pretend partners and invoices (the boxes, the late list, the search, Open it, the card tags, the side bar number). **The partner's own page changes (colours, days, Pick all ready, Enter, Settings) were not seen running, and nothing was seen on the live app.** If opening Partners feels slow, it is the one `partner_ledger` call for each partner (three at a time): move it into one database function.

## Receivables: payments in the bank with no invoice

- (Carlo, Nov 9.) **Only 2026 and forward** (his rule; older years are left off the list, the cut-off is in `bank_unapplied`). Money that came into the bank (kinds Deposits, Card & online payments in, Partners & financing, Other) with nothing attached on the Bank page. Receivables shows Eco Man at the top: "Carlo, please help me find these. There are 496 payments in the bank with no invoice." → **Show me the list** opens a **side bar on the right** of Receivables, not a new page (Carlo, Nov 9; `#ua-side`; it is narrow, 440px, and the page moves over beside it, `body.ua-open #app`, so the page can still be used to look up the invoice while the side bar is open; it stays open on every page, so customers and invoices can be searched, until its Close square is pressed; the Receivables Tools panel then opens just to its left, so both show; with both open they take no more than 40% of the screen together, 20% each, and the page keeps 60%: Carlo's rule) (`openUnapplied`, `UA`, `uaCardHtml` in `index.html`): month squares across the top (All months, then each month with its count; `p_month`), one big card per payment (amount, day, what the bank says), up to 3 invoices for exactly the same amount as "Yes, link this one" squares, a box to type an invoice number and **Save the link**, Undo after. One payment can take several invoices: type the numbers with a comma between them, or add another after a link (the card stays, "Add another invoice to this payment"). The link is a `bank_invoice_links` row (how `by hand`), the same as the Bank page makes, so it shows on the Bank page right away; it does NOT record a payment on the invoice. RPCs `bank_unapplied`, `bank_unapplied_link`, `bank_unapplied_unlink` (`_recv_ok`: invoices.adjust, owner/admin/accounting or bank.view), `sync/extra/241_bank_unapplied.sql`. Most of the list is Elavon card deposits (many invoices in one deposit): those are really matched by loading the Credit Card Statement on the Bank page.

- **It is a square now, next to Check invoices** (Carlo, Nov 11, 8:42 a.m.: "on the owing page make this [Carlo, please help me find these. There are 496 payments in the bank with no invoice. 🔎 Show me the list] a box as well, next to the Check invoices one"). The Eco Man box under the squares is gone (`uaBoxHtml` now only leaves an empty, hidden `#ua-box`). The fourth square on "What do you want to do?" is **🔎 Find bank payments**, with "496 payments in the bank have no invoice. Help me find them." under it (`uaSqHtml(big)`, `data-ua-sq`, `data-ua-open`: the same click as before, it opens the side bar list). Once a job is picked it stays in the small row as "Find bank payments (496)". The number is filled in when the count comes back and after the side bar is closed (`uaSqFresh`); the square is not drawn when there are none. ❓ Help has a step for it. Where the notes above say Receivables "shows Eco Man at the top", read this. Checked: the script parses and the row was looked at in a test picture. **Not seen on the live app.**

- **The four totals stay on the screen after one is clicked** (Carlo, Nov 11, 8:46 a.m.: clicking Everything owed to us / They should have paid by now / The work is not done yet / Total paid to us "opens up other options; I do not want these ones to disappear, it's hard to keep track of what is happening"). A click on a total starts Chase money, and that job used to hide the totals row (`.ow-summary`). Now the row stays in Chase money, above "1 Pick who", and the picked total has a yellow frame; a click on another total switches to it. CSS only (the `ow-mode-chase` hide rule). The totals are still hidden in Record a payment and Check invoices, which do not start from a total. **Not seen on the live app.**

- **A click on a total no longer leaves a blank band at the top** (Carlo, Nov 11, 8:48 a.m., with a picture: "now when I hit it the top of the screen is blank; everything should stay where it is unless it has to move"). Cause: the click shrank the big squares to the small row, and the app-wide **"Nothing moves" rule** (the click handler under that heading in `index.html`: it holds whatever was clicked at the same spot on the screen, and at the very top of a page it does that by padding the top of the page) then pushed everything down by the height that was lost. Now a job started from a total keeps the top exactly as it was (`OW.bigTop`: the question, the big squares with Chase money framed, the totals), and only steps 1, 2, 3 open under the totals; the line under the squares changes to "Click another total to see who owes that one. ⬅ Start over" (the same height as the line it replaces; a Start over square in the row made the row wrap and the top taller, so it is a link in that line). Picking a square still gives the small row (`owSetMode` clears `bigTop`), and the job squares are left out of the "Nothing moves" rule (`.ow-what`), because that page goes to its top by itself. **Lesson for other pages: when a click makes the part ABOVE the clicked thing shorter, that rule pads the top. Keep what is above a clicked thing the same height.** Checked: the script parses, and in a test page the top block is the same height before and after a total is clicked. **The click itself was not run, and it was not seen on the live app.**

- **Chase money has four steps: 1 Pick who · 2 Pick by year and month · 3 Pick how late · 4 The list** (Carlo, Nov 11, 8:54 a.m.: "now 2 Pick how late, make that 3 and make 2 Pick by year and month"). The new step 2 is a teal panel between Pick who and Pick how late (`.ow-ym-panel`, `.ow-scopes-ym`, `data-ow-ym`): **Show all** (every year) and one box for each year that has invoices, newest first, each with its dollars and how many invoices; picking a year opens a second row, "Months of 2026", with one box for each month that has invoices. A picked month clicked again goes back to its whole year. It goes by the invoice's own date (a job still booked: the day it is booked for; `owYmOf`). `OW.ym` = null, "2026" or "2026-03"; `owYmF` narrows step 3's boxes (inside `owPtnF`) and the list (in `renderOwing`), and the "You are looking at" bar names it (`owYmName`). It does **not** change the totals at the top or the amounts in step 1. Picking another total or another "who" clears it (the steps under a changed step start again, as the ages already did); picking a year or month clears the age. Only in Chase money, and not for Total paid to us (then the steps are 1, 2, 3 as before: `ymOn`). ❓ Help has the new step. Where the notes above say "1 Pick who → 2 Pick how late → 3 The list", read this. Checked: the script parses; the panel was built from the real code with pretend invoices and looked at in a test picture; the filter gave 1 of 20 for one month. **The clicks and the list on the real page were not run, and it was not seen on the live app.**

- **The four totals are square app boxes** (Carlo, Nov 11, 8:58 a.m.: "turn these into 4 square app boxes"). Everything owed to us (💰), They should have paid by now (⏰), The work is not done yet (🛠) are dark blue squares the same size as the job squares above them (230px wide, at least 210px high), and Total paid to us (✅) is a green one: a picture on top, the name, the dollars big, then the two small lines (how many invoices; customers + partners). The picked one has the yellow frame. CSS only on the same buttons (`.ow-summary .ow-sumbtn`, `data-ow-scope`), so the clicks did not change. They are taller than the flat boxes were, so steps 1 to 4 start lower on the page. Looked at in a test picture with the app's styles. **Not seen on the live app.**

- **The small words sit at the bottom of the large boxes** (Carlo, Nov 11, 9:04 a.m.: "on the Chase money button move the text under Chase money to the bottom of the box so there is more room between them; do it for all the large boxes"). In the four big job squares the small line ("See who owes us and how late they are"…) is at the bottom of the square (`.ow-what-sub { margin-top: auto }`), with the picture and the name at the top. I did the same in the four totals squares: the small lines (how many invoices; customers + partners) are at the bottom, the picture, name and dollars at the top. CSS only. The small squares in the row have no small line, so they did not change. Looked at in a test picture. **Not seen on the live app.**

- **Send reminder emails is a button under each month and under each how late box** (Carlo, Nov 11, 9:13 a.m.: "move the send reminder emails from the right side bar and make it a button under each month in RE 10 so you can send all emails to that group in that month, also make it a button in Pick how late squares as well"). The Reminder emails panel is gone from ⚙ Tools. In Chase money, step 2: each month box has a dark blue **✉ Send reminder emails** button under it (`.ow-cell` wraps the box and the button; `owRemBtn(sel, rows, who)`, `.ow-rem-btn`, `data-ow-remind="ym:2026-03"`; the month's rows are kept in `OW.remSets` when the step is drawn, so they follow who is picked in step 1). Step 3: each age box (Current, 30, 45, 60, 90, 120+ days) has the same button (`data-ow-remind="age30"`…); with a year or month picked in step 2 it is only that age in that year or month (`owAgeRows` already follows `OW.ym`). No button on the year boxes, on Show all, on "Not due yet", or on a box with nothing to send. Only invoices with money owing, with an invoice, and not waiting on booked work (`owRemOk`). The click opens the same list as before (`openOwRemind`: tick who gets it, check the email, "Send them?" first, test mode respected); nothing about the emails changed. The old "everyone over 30 days" button is gone with the panel (`over30` still works in the code). ❓ Help has a step. Checked: the script parses; the month boxes with the button were looked at in a test picture. **The age boxes were not looked at, the click was not run, and it was not seen on the live app.** Right after (9:24 a.m.: "make the send reminder smaller text so it fits better") the button's words are 12px (they were 14px), with less space around them. Then at 9:26 a.m. ("make the boxes bigger and add the send reminder email to the box but make sure all the text can be read"): the month boxes and the how late boxes are bigger (at least 225px wide, bigger words: name 15px, dollars 23px, small line 13.5px) and the button sits **inside the box, at its bottom** (14px words, they may wrap to two lines). A button can not be put inside a button, so `.ow-cell` is `position: relative`, the box gets room at its bottom (`padding-bottom: 58px` when the cell has a button) and `.ow-rem-btn` is placed over that room (`position: absolute`). Where this note says "under" the box and 12px, read this. The month boxes were looked at in a test picture; the how late boxes were not.

- **Clean up old balances is at the top, next to the word Receivables** (Carlo, Nov 11, 9:29 a.m.: "on the right side bar move the Clean up old balances to the top of the page next to Receivables text"). A dark blue **🧹 Clean up old balances ›** button beside the page's heading (`.ow-what-top` holds the `h1` and the button; the same `#ow-cleanup`, so the click is the same), on the first screen and in every job. It is gone from ⚙ Tools → More things. Checked: the script parses. **Not looked at in a picture and not seen on the live app.**

- **Which invoices, More things and When are gone from the Tools side bar** (Carlo, Nov 11, 9:30 a.m.: "remove this from the side bar as well", with the three panels pasted). Not drawn any more: **Which invoices** (All invoices / Paid invoices / Not paid invoices / Booked jobs), **More things** (Don't show $0 invoices, Copy all, Open as a full page) and the **When** date picker. ⚙ Tools now holds only Find an invoice by its amount. So that nothing they set stays on with no way to change it, the page always starts on All time, with $0 invoices showing, and never on the all / paid / booked lists (the three lines under `OW.scope = OW.scope || "owing"`). Their handlers and helper code are still in `index.html` (`owDateTiles`, `#ow-zero`, `#ow-copy`, `#ow-fullpage`, scopes `all|paid|booked`), so any of them can be put back somewhere else. I took "remove" as take them off, not move them. Checked: the script parses. **Not seen on the live app.**

- **Find by amount is a permanent right side bar on Receivables** (Carlo, Nov 11, 9:33 a.m.: "make the side bar with find by amount a permanent right side bar that moves with the page so it can always be used"). The old Tools panel (`.ow-tools`, now also `.ow-tools-perm`) is always drawn, with no Tools square and no Close: heading "🔎 Find by amount", then `owAmtHtml()`. On screens 1000px and wider it is fixed to the right edge, 400px wide, full height, so it stays put while the page scrolls, and the page gets narrower beside it (`body:has(article.owing .ow-tools-perm) #app { width: calc(100vw - 400px) }`). With the bank payments side bar open too, the older rule still holds (20% each, the page 60%). Under 1000px it is a plain box in the page, not fixed. `OW.tools` and the `#ow-tools` / `#ow-tools-x` handlers are left in the code but nothing uses them. ❓ Help's last step points at it. Where the notes above say "⚙ Tools square", read this. Checked: the script parses. **Not looked at in a picture and not seen on the live app: the results table has seven columns and may need sideways scrolling inside 400px; if so make the bar wider or the table simpler.** Right after (9:36 a.m.: "make it start in the middle of the side bar so there is a space above it and in the space have it say find your invoice by amounts"): the top of the bar is an open space, about half the screen high (`.ow-tools-space`, `min-height: 46vh`), with a big 🔎 and the words **"Find your invoice by amounts."** in its middle; the amount box starts under it. The small "🔎 Find by amount" heading is gone (the space says it). The space is in `ECO_IDLE`. On a narrow screen the space is only as tall as its words.

- **The Find by amount side bar is four numbered steps, one under the other** (Carlo, Nov 11, 9:38 a.m.: "now organize the side bar better so it's easier to understand"). `owAmtHtml` (the form part only; the search itself and `wireOwAmt` did not change, the ids are the same): **1 Type the amount** (one big box) · **2 How should I look?** (the six ways, stacked, in plain words, the picked one dark blue with a tick: "One invoice for exactly this amount", "Two invoices of one customer that add up to it", Three / Four / Five "invoices that add up to it", "Close to this amount, a little more or less"; then "How much more or less? $" or "Which amounts do I add up?" when that way needs it; `AF_MODES`) · **3 Which dates?** "You can skip this step." (A month / Or from / to) · a big green **🔎 Find the invoices** and, after a search, **✕ Clear and start again** · **4 What I found** (only after a search). The found invoices are **one card each**, not a wide table, so nothing runs off the side: invoice number, date, customer, job type, then "Invoice total", "Paid so far", "Still owing" (and "Off by") with the amount on the right (CSS on the same table: `.ow-af-res`, labels by `td:nth-child(5…8)::before`; the column headings, and so sorting by a heading, are hidden). Classes `.ow-af`, `.ow-af-step`, `.ow-af-h`, `.ow-af-n`, `.ow-af-modes`, `.ow-af-l`, `.ow-af-go`. Checked: the script parses; the real function was drawn in a test picture with two pretend invoices ("close to" way). **The pair and group results were not looked at, no real search was run, and it was not seen on the live app.**

- **Always four squares across, beside the side bar, on any screen** (Carlo, Nov 11, 9:42 a.m.: "I always want staff to see 4 buttons with the side bar wide regardless of their screen size, shrink them if needed to make it fit, and the side bar should never be bigger than anything else"). The four big job squares (`.ow-what-row4`) and the four totals (`.ow-summary`) are each a grid of four columns (up to 230px each) that never wraps: on a smaller screen the squares, their pictures and their words shrink with the room there is (`container-type: inline-size` on the row, sizes in `cqw` inside `clamp()`; smallest words about 10 to 12px). So that the row holds only the four squares, **❓ Help moved up beside the heading**, next to Clean up old balances (same `#ow-help`; `extra` in `owWhatHtml` is empty now). **The side bar is `min(360px, 24vw)` wide** (it was 400px): never wider than the left side bar (360px) and always under a quarter of the screen, so the page is always the widest part; it is a side bar from 700px wide and up (it was 1000px), a plain box in the page under that. The small squares row (after a job square is picked) is as before. Lesson kept: the top block's height changed (Help left the row), the same in both states, so the "Nothing moves" rule is not upset. Checked: the script parses; the first screen with the totals was looked at in test pictures at 1100px and 520px of page width: four across in both, all words readable. **Not seen on the live app, and not looked at with the left side bar, the page and the side bar all together.**

- **The Find by amount side bar: only two things to do at first** (Carlo, Nov 11, 9:47 a.m.: "the side bar is still a bit hard to understand"; he did not say which part, so I cut it down to the least a person needs). The space at the top now also says what it is for: "Money came in and you do not know which invoice it is for? Type the amount below. I will find the invoice." Then **1 Type the amount of the payment** · **2 Press the green button** (🔎 Find the invoice, inside the step; ✕ Clear and start again after a search) · **3 What I found** (after a search). The six ways to look and the dates are no longer steps: they are closed under one dashed button, **"▸ Did not find it? Try another way."** (`data-ow-amt-more`, `OW.amtMore`), which opens "How should I look?" (the six ways), "Only look in some dates?" and "Then press the green button again." It opens by a click, **by itself when nothing was found**, and stays open while a way other than exact or a date is picked (`more` in `owAmtHtml`). Clear and start again goes back to the exact way and closes it. Where the note above says four steps with "2 How should I look?" and "3 Which dates?", read this. ## Bank page: the Find by amount side bar, and attach what it finds (Nov 12)

## A Cancel button on an open P.O. and on a Credit Memo (Nov 13)

- **A P.O.'s own page** (`renderPOPage`): top right, a soft red **✕ Cancel this P.O.** (`#pop-cancel`; people who can change the P.O., not on one already cancelled). It asks with two squares (Yes, cancel it / No, keep it), then saves the P.O. with status Cancelled through the page's own save (`po_full_save`, reason "Cancelled from the P.O.'s page"), so it is **kept on file, not deleted**, and anything else typed on the page is saved with it. A cancelled P.O. shows "This P.O. is cancelled." there, with **Cancelled by mistake? Put it back** (it goes back to Not sent). A supplier invoice tied to it stays tied; the question says so. A P.O. from ServiceTitan can be cancelled too, but ServiceTitan's nightly update can change its status back.
- **A Credit Memo** (it is opened on its supplier credit paper; `sinvMemoTile`): under the green Credit Memo made box, the small link "Not a credit? Take it off" is now a soft red button, **✕ Cancel this Credit Memo** (`.sci-memo-x`; the same `credit_memo_remove`). It asks first. Only the memo is cancelled (its record is removed and written in the history); the supplier's credit paper stays, and the memo can be made again. **A memo that answered a Credit Order has no button**, as before: the Credit Order is undone on its invoice first.

## Payroll: Source payment to the CRA, and pay rates (George, Nov 13)

- (George, Nov 13: "take a look at section PA1… build a Source payment into this section that tells me what the source is and due by when… mark it as paid… if I paid the amount… from the timesheets or what my accountant said… a reminder for George and Carlo… someone to say it was paid and attach the payment confirmation pdf". Then: "no email reminders, go ahead".)
- **Source payment box** at the top of the Payroll page (PA 1 area, under the heading; owners only): one card per half month, made by the app from Oct 16, 2026 on (`_src_ensure`): 1st–15th due the 25th, 16th–end due the 10th of the next month (CRA twice a month, threshold 1); a Saturday/Sunday due date moves to the Monday (holidays are not moved: the accountant's due date wins when pasted). Status: Not due yet / Due in N days (orange, from 3 days before) / Late: N days (red) / Scheduled for <date> / ✓ Paid. Periods not over yet are folded under "This period"; paid ones are a table under "Paid" (📄 PDF, Open).
- **📋 Paste the accountant's email**: reads Remitting Period, "pay online for <date>" (due), Gross Payroll, Total tax/CPP/EI, Employees, Payroll Account No (`srcReadEmail`). Saved with `source_save`.
- **✓ Mark it paid (attach the PDF)**: the bank's Government Payment & Filing confirmation PDF is required (`source_mark_paid` refuses without the PDF and a confirmation number). Read in the browser with pdf.js (`srcReadPdf`, `pdfLinesY`): confirmation #, status ("To be processed"), payment date, due date, date paid to employees, amount, gross, employees, account. Checks shown: paid vs the accountant's amount, payment date after the due date (late), the PDF's due date / employee pay date not fitting the period, PDF gross vs email gross. A payment date in the future = Scheduled. "Take the payment off" (`source_unpay`) for a wrong PDF.
- **🧮 Check the gross against our timesheets** (`source_gross_estimate`): the pay days in the period (every second Thursday from Oct 22, 2026; each pays Monday to Sunday ending 4 days before). Techs: each day first On the way/arrived to last Done; office: time in to out held to 15 min either side of their expected hours; × the pay rate on the last day worked (salary = a year ÷ 26). No overtime, commission, vacation pay, stats, pick-up time or non-job time: it is an estimate, shown as such, with each person and who has no rate. It checks the **gross**, not the tax (tax/CPP/EI depend on each person's TD1 and year-to-date limits).
- **Reminders (owners = George and Carlo, rule number one), no email** (George said no email): Tasks page box "💵 Source payment to the CRA" (`srcTaskBox`), counted in the Tasks number (`showFollowCount`), and a pop-up once a day on each computer (`srcNag`, localStorage `reid.src.nag`) while one is due within 3 days or late and not marked paid with the PDF (`source_due`).
- **Pay rates**: there was nowhere to enter them (the rates came once from ServiceTitan's staff export, `employees.hourly_wage`; the hourly sync does not bring them). Now each person's payroll page (tech and office) has **💲 Pay rate** for owners: By the hour / Salary (a year), amount, Starts on; history with Remove (`staff_pay_rates`, `pay_rate_list`, `pay_rate_set`, `pay_rate_del`, `_pay_rate_on`: newest rate started on or before a day, else ServiceTitan's wage). The Payroll list shows each rate (`payroll_people` now has `rate`) and a red line of people (not owners) with no rate.
- **The payroll account number is only in the database** (`source_remits.account_no`), never in this file or the code: the repo is public. Same for the PDFs (`source_remits.pdf`, base64).
- Database: `sync/extra/275_source_payments.sql`. Checked: the SQL was run on a test Postgres with pretend staff and timesheets (periods made, due dates incl. a Sunday → Monday, save, mark paid → scheduled, reminders list empty after, gross estimate by hand-checked hours, non-owners get nothing); both readers were run on George's real email text and PDF; the box, the pay pop-up and the pay rate box were drawn with the app's styles in a test browser and looked at. **Not run against the live database and not seen on the live app.** George could not push (no write access for this session); Carlo has to push it.

- **Older payments, and a PDF always goes on its own period** (George, Nov 13, with a picture: Paid (2), the Nov 1–15 card paid Oct 26 with confirmation 9btfm-0191707: "I am trying to add older paid pdf files and it appears the system will not let me add anymore, it stopped me at 2"). The app only made cards from Oct 16, 2026, so there was no card for an older PDF, and he put an Oct 26 payment on the Nov 1–15 card (the only open one). Now: **＋ Add older payments** in the dark blue bar of the box (`srcOlderBox`): pick one or many confirmation PDFs; each is read and saved on its own period (RPC `source_add_paid`, `sync/extra/276_source_older.sql`), with a line for each file (✓ period, amount, date, confirmation, late; ✗ and why). **The period comes from the PDF**: the date paid to employees (`_src_period_of`), else its due date (`_src_period_of_due`: due on or after the 20th → the 1st–15th of that month; earlier → the 16th–end of the month before), else the card. A period that already has another payment is refused ("Take that payment off first"). **Mark it paid on a card uses the same save**, so a PDF for another period goes on that period, and the box says so before Save (`srcPerOfPdf`). `source_mark_paid` is no longer called by the page. **Fix in 276** (runs on each update, idempotent): a paid row whose own date paid to employees is outside its period is moved to its own period (made if needed; amounts that came only from the PDF go with it; a note says it was moved), unless that period already has a payment. If his Nov 1–15 PDF had no date paid to employees read from it, the fix does not move it: Open it and Take the payment off, then add it again. Checked on a test Postgres: the wrong row moved to Oct 1–15; a PDF with only a due date went to its period; a second PDF for a paid period was refused; the period helpers in Node. **Not seen on the live app.**

- **A PDF that can not be read fully is shown beside the boxes to check** (George, Nov 13, with a picture: 8 of 13 saved, five "✗ … not saved. I could not find the confirmation number, the payment date, the amount, the period on it", e.g. "24-8-30-Source-Government Payment & Filing Service 42699.85 to be paid Sep15.pdf": "why is it not accepting them? maybe it should open the pdf and show me the issue and then I can confirm what needs to be done"). They most likely have no words in them (a picture PDF), so nothing was found. Now in `srcReadPdf(f, say)`: (1) the PDF's own words (`srcParseConf`, more forgiving: OCR spaces in the confirmation number, dates as "2024 Aug. 30" or 2024-08-30); (2) when the confirmation, payment date or amount is still missing, its first 3 pages are read as pictures (`smOcr`, about 15 s a page; the line says "This PDF is a picture…"); (3) the file name fills what is still missing (`srcFromName`: "24-8-30-…" = employees paid Aug 30, 2024, "42699.85" = the amount, "to be paid Sep15" = the payment date; marked as guessed, `o.fromName`). In **＋ Add older payments**, a file that is complete is saved as before; **any other file (something missing, or anything taken from the file name) is not saved by itself: it becomes a card under the list** (`fixCard` in `srcOlderBox`, `.src-fix`): the PDF itself on the left (`iframe` of the file), on the right the boxes Confirmation number, Date paid to employees (sets the period), Due date, Payment date, Amount paid, filled with what was read; empty or guessed boxes have a red edge; "It goes on Jan 16 – 31, 2024"; **Save this payment** (only when the confirmation, payment date, amount and period are there) or **Skip this one**. A save error (period already paid) shows on the card. The summary line says "8 saved. 5 need you: each one is below, with its PDF…" in red, or "✓ 13 saved. Nothing is left to check." in green (it used to say "8 of 13 saved. You can pick more, or Close." in green). The pop-up is wider for this (`.src-pop-wide`, 1180px). Mark it paid on one card also uses the picture reading. Checked: the script parses; the readers were run in Node on his real Nov 13 PDF's words, on made-up OCR words and on his file names (24-8-30 … 42699.85 … Sep15 → Aug 30, 2024, $42,699.85, Sep 15, 2024; 24-12-31 … Jan15 → Jan 15, 2025); the card was drawn with the app's styles in a test picture. **I did not have his five PDFs, so I do not know what is in them; the picture reading in the browser and the live app were not run or seen.**

- **Paid is in year folders, with month totals, year to date, year over year and sales** (George, Nov 13: "once I open the paid collapsible menu it should have the items in year folders… a running menu of how much source I paid that year and each month and then year to date and a year over year showing the amounts either plus or minus… if we are actually within a reasonable spend for payroll in comparison to the same time frames for the money made in accounting"). Under **Paid (n)** (`srcYearsHtml`, `.src-year`): one folder per year, newest first, the newest open (open/closed is remembered while the app is open: `SRC.paidOpen`, `SRC.yearOpen`). The folder's line: the year, "Source paid $… (so far)", **Year to date vs Jan–Nov of last year** (this year) or **vs last year** (older years) as +$/−$ and %, how many payments, "⚠ N missing". Inside: a month table (Month · Source paid · Same month last year (+/−) · Gross payroll · Sales before HST · Payroll % of sales · Source % of sales) with a **Year to date** (or Year) row, then that year's payments. Up = orange, down = green (more source paid is the thing to watch). RPC **`source_summary()`** (`sync/extra/279_source_summary.sql`): for each month from a year before the first payment to this month, the source paid (paid with PDF; `coalesce(paid_amount, total)`), the gross payroll from the email/PDF, and **sales = `sum(invoices.subtotal)`** by invoice date for invoices not void or draft, not paid at the store and not a store's copy (the same filter as `tax_periods` on the Tax payments page; I did not see `tax_summary`'s own sum, so its Sales figure may differ a little). A payment counts in the month its period starts. Loaded after the box is drawn (`srcSumLoad`).
- **Open / ✕ Close on a paid line** (George: "you gave me an Open button but no way to close the opened file"): Open shows the payment's card under its line and turns into **✕ Close**; the card also has a ✕ Close at its top right (`data-src-close`).
- **Missing confirmations** (George: "I want to know what confirmations are missing from the first one uploaded, noted for us to update"): `srcMissing()` lists every half month from the first paid period to the last one that has ended with no record at all (a period with its own unpaid card is shown as that card). A red box at the top of the Source payment box lists them ("⚠ 5 periods have no payment confirmation on file … ＋ Add the missing ones"; "If one was never paid, that is a CRA payment still owing"), each year folder says "⚠ N missing", and each missing period is a red line in that year's payments with ＋ Add it (opens Add older payments). It is worked out in the browser each time, so a period disappears from the list as soon as its PDF is saved.
- Checked: the script parses; `source_summary` was run on a test Postgres (void and draft invoices left out); the box with three years of pretend payments and sales, four missing periods and the month table was drawn with the app's styles in a test picture. **The Open/Close click was not run and nothing was seen on the live app.**

## Office staff time (Payroll)

- Office staff = app role office, admin, accounting, dispatcher or warehouse (not techs, even if ServiceTitan says technician). Their payroll page is time in / time out, not calls (`openOfficePayroll`, `sync/extra/212_office_time.sql`).
- Work day turns over at 2 am. Time in: "Let's start the day" screen, or signing in in the morning. Time out: the "Done work" button at the top of the side bar; if they forget, their last typing/click/scroll, and they must verify it next time.
- Pay counts only from 15 min before the expected start to 15 min after the expected end. Owners set expected hours per weekday on the person's payroll page.

## Dispatch areas (how places are coloured)

- Places are coloured ONE way everywhere (customer page, dispatch board, map, Calls nearby): 8 dispatch areas from the postal code's first 3 characters (`DISPATCH_AREAS` / `areaOf()` at the top of the main script in `index.html`). Rural K0A and unlisted postal areas go to the nearest area by map location; over 35 km from all of them is "Out of town". Agreed with Carlo Nov 7, 2026.
- West: Kanata & Stittsville (blue) · Nepean (green) · Barrhaven, Riverside South & Manotick (orange) · Central (purple) · South Ottawa (red) · Gloucester & Vanier (teal) · Orléans & east (yellow) · Out of town (grey).
- Neighbourhoods, communities and towns are text only now, not colours. Don't bring back a second "border" colour.

## Dispatch board: what ServiceTitan's board shows and ours did not (George's email, Nov 10)

- (George emailed Carlo at 8:37 p.m. with four pictures of Mon Nov 9: our board had Adonay on "Abu Bakar 2 pm" while ServiceTitan's board did not, and ServiceTitan showed grey events, "George" on Adonay and "off sick today" on Dayton, that ours did not. Carlo: "help me with this email George sent".) Both were about what the board **shows**; the sync had the right data.
- **A call whose job is on hold in ServiceTitan is off our board.** ServiceTitan takes a held appointment off the tech's line; our sync keeps the visit as `scheduled` with its tech (appointment status Hold → `scheduled`, job status Hold → `on_hold`), so the board kept drawing it (job 155562365). `dispatch_day` (with a date) and `dispatch_range` now leave out a visit when `j.status = 'on_hold' AND v.status = 'scheduled' AND j.st_id IS NOT NULL` (written into their own files, `sync/extra/011_dispatch_walks.sql` and `077_dispatch_range.sql`, and patched live). Only 3 such visits between Nov 3 and Nov 20. The job itself is untouched and still shows on the customer's page. A job put on hold in our own app (no `st_id`) still shows.
- **The grey events on the techs' ServiceTitan schedules show on our board** (off sick, meetings, holds: `st_nonjob_appts`, already copied every hour for payroll). `staff_blocks_list` (`sync/extra/078_staff_blocks.sql`, live) now adds the active ones to the busy times it returns (`st: true`, id negative, `can_delete` false), so they are on the day board (grey 📅 box on the tech's line, `.tl-block-st` in `dspBlocksHtml`), the week and month views, and where a tech's free time is checked for booking. The 3:30 a.m. "CALL PAST THIS ARE FOR THE NEXT DAY!" marker every tech has is left out (anything that ends by 6 a.m.). They are as fresh as the hourly events sync.
- **An event and a call at the same time both show, side by side** (Carlo, Nov 12, 4:03 p.m.: "when an event is on the dispatch board and it is during another appointment have them both show, just reduce the time for each so they fit; I do not want the event to be under the appointment or it will get missed"). On the day board a tech's event (the grey 📅 ServiceTitan ones and the ⛔ blocks made in the app) used to be drawn under any call at the same time. Now, on the tech's line (`dspBlockFit`, `dspBlockSpans`, `dspBlocksHtml(id, pos, timed)` in `dspTimeline`'s `row`): **the call starts first** → the call is drawn only up to where the event starts, then the event at its own time (a call 12 to 3 with an event 1 to 2 shows the call 12 to 1 and the event 1 to 2); **the event starts first** by half an hour or more → the call is as it was and the event shows in the time in front of it (and after it, if the event goes on: an all-day "off sick" fills every gap between the calls); **they start together** (less than half an hour apart) → they share the time: the event gets the front half (an hour at most), the call the rest. Only how long each is drawn changes: the words on the call and on the event still say the real times, nothing is changed in the booking, and hovering an event that is drawn shorter says so. If calls cover an event's whole time, the event is drawn full length as before (under them). Day board only (the week and month views list things, they do not stack). Checked: the script parses; the rule was run in Node on five pretend cases. **Not looked at in a picture and not seen on the live app.**
- **An event box has the same shape as a call box, in grey** (Carlo, Nov 12, 4:27 p.m.: "make the event box the same shape as the others but keep it grey in colour"). On the day board an event used to be a shorter box with a dashed edge, stripes, rounder corners and its words on one line. Now (`.dsp .tl-track .tl-block`, one CSS block after the `.dsp .tl-card` rules; `tlb-name` / `tlb-sub` in `dspBlocksHtml`): the same height as a call (top 3px, bottom 3px of the line), the same corners, a thin edge with a thick left edge, the name in bold on the first line and the time under it. A ServiceTitan event (📅) is dark grey with white words; one made in the app (⛔) is light grey with dark words, no stripes. It still can not be dragged or clicked like a call. Looked at in a test picture beside call boxes (same top, height, corners and left edge). **Not seen on the live app.**
- **No call box or event box is ever smaller than half an hour of the board** (Carlo, Nov 12, 4:32 p.m.: "take a look at Mina's first call on November 12, Dorine Dounla; do not ever let a call or event box become smaller than that size, make that the min size; if need shrink other calls before or after to do it"). That day Mina had two calls booked 8 to 11 and the next at 9, so the two shared the hour: Dorine's box was **half an hour wide**. That is the smallest now (`DSP_MIN = 0.5`, `dspMinFit(boxes, h0, h1)`). On a tech's line, the calls drawn one by one or side by side (the board's usual "fit" way) and the event pieces are gathered in `boxes` in `dspTimeline`'s `row`, then: a box under the size grows to it; the boxes after it start later, so they get shorter, and one only moves when it is at the smallest size itself; if that runs past the end of the board, the boxes before give the room back. (`dspBlockFit` also uses `DSP_MIN` for what it leaves a call beside an event.) Three calls at the same time with the next an hour later used to be 20 minutes wide each; now each is half an hour and the next call's box starts half an hour later and is shorter. **Only the drawing changes: the words on each box still say the real times.** With many calls at one time a box can sit later on the line than its real time (four at 8:00 run to 10:00). Not changed: the other ways to show overlaps in the board's settings (thin lanes, a stack, a summary, grow the row) and the Any time column. Checked: the script parses; `dspMinFit` was run in Node on five cases (Mina's day comes out as it was). **Not looked at in a picture and not seen on the live app.**
- **A call that is done can not be moved or changed from the board** (Carlo, Nov 12, 4:40 p.m.: "once a call is completed there should be no more option to move or edit the call from the dispatch board"). A completed call's box can not be picked up and dragged (`draggable="false"` in `dspCard` and `dspTlCard`; the mouse is a hand, not a grab), and `dropCall` refuses it ("This call is done. It can not be moved any more."), so a box dragged out of a stack is stopped too. A click still opens its details, which already had no Change time, Techs or Status for a done call; they now say "✓ This call is done. It can not be moved or changed from the board any more." **I kept one way back, for owners only**: "Marked done by mistake? Put it back on the schedule" (it was there for everyone); told him, his call to take it off. A cancelled call still has "Put it back on the schedule" for everyone. Still in a done call's details: Open the customer file, Book another visit on this job, Add to a partner list, and the address's second zone (that is about the address, not the call). The customer's page and the invoice are not locked by this. Checked: the script parses. **Not run, not looked at in a picture and not seen on the live app.**
- **Nothing from the past can be moved on the board** (Carlo, Nov 12, 4:44 p.m.: "do the same rule for notes as well from the past"; 4:46 p.m.: "so understand nothing should be movable from the past on the dispatch board"). "Past" = a day before today (`dspToday`, `dspPast(r)`). **A call on a past day**, done or not: its box can not be dragged (`draggable="false"`), `dropCall` refuses it, and its details have no Change time, no Techs and no Optimize suggestion; they say "This call is on a day in the past. It can not be moved from the board any more. If the work still has to be done, press Book another visit on this job." Status is still there for a past call that is not done (Booked / On the way / On site, Needs another visit, Cancel), so it can be closed off. **A note or a held spot on a past day** (`openBoardNote` when `DSP.date` is before today): a click opens it to read only, "A note from the past", every box greyed, no Save and no Delete, one Close button. A note that repeats can still be changed from today's board. No owner way around it. Not locked: adding a new note on a past day, and typing a past date in Change time on a call of today. **I first read "notes" as the customer page's notes and locked Edit / Delete on old ones there (commit 1a1a59b); that was wrong and was taken back off in this change.** Only what the screen offers; the database functions were not changed. Checked: the script parses. **Not run, not looked at in a picture and not seen on the live app.**
- **"Past calls not complete yet" at the top of the board, and a note's change counts from today on** (Carlo, Nov 12, 4:50 p.m.: "let the note change but do not change it in the past, only on current and future; any calls from the past that were not done should be moved to a section called past calls not complete yet, somewhere at the top of the board"). **This changes two things in the note just above.**
  - **The section**: on today's board and on any day after it, above Waiting for a day, a red-edged strip **Past calls not complete yet** with a count (`details.dsp-wait.dsp-pastopen`, `DSP.pastOpen`, loaded in `loadDispatch` by RPC `dispatch_past_open(p_today, p_days 30)`): every call booked for a day in the **last 30 days** that is still Booked / On the way / On site, whose job is not done, cancelled or on hold (22 on Nov 12; older ones, hundreds of left-overs from ServiceTitan back to 2021, are left out: I chose 30 days). Each card has the day it was booked for in front. **These can be moved** (`r.pastOpen`; `dspPast` is false for them): drag one onto a tech and it is booked for the day on the screen, at the same time of day as before, with that tech in the lead's place (`dropCall`); or click it for Change time (the day box starts on the day on the screen), Techs and Status. Once moved, marked done or cancelled it leaves the section. On its own past day the call is still drawn and still can not be moved; its details say to move it from the section. The list is loaded when the board is loaded, not by the 30-second check.
  - **Notes and held spots**: one on a past day is still read only there. **One that repeats can be changed or deleted from today's board (or a later day), and that counts from today on**: RPC `board_note_change(p_id, p, p_today)` stops the old one yesterday (`repeat_until`) and makes a new one from today with the change (its first day is the next day the repeat lands on; days skipped from today on are carried over); `board_note_stop(p_id, p_today)` is "Delete it from today on" (the old one stops yesterday; the past days keep it). A note whose first day is today or later is changed and deleted as before. The pop-up says "This one repeats. A change counts from today on. The days in the past stay as they were."
  - Database: `sync/extra/264_board_past.sql` (live Nov 12). Checked: the script parses; the three functions were made; the count of past calls was read with plain SQL. **The functions could not be run as a signed-in person, so the section, the drag, and a note change were not run or seen, and nothing was seen on the live app.**
- **Past calls not complete yet and Waiting for a day look like the No tech yet line** (Carlo, Nov 12, 5:01 p.m.: "make the Past calls not complete yet and the Waiting for a day look like the No tech yet and add scroll bars when needed"). Each is one line the height of a tech's line (CSS on `.dsp details.dsp-wait`, one block after the older `.dsp-wait` rules): the same thin edge, corners and light red wash as No tech yet; a name box on the left, 154px wide so the calls start where the board's time line starts, with the name in bold and "22 calls" under it (`.dw-sub`; the old count pill and the hint line are hidden); the calls in one row beside it, each with its small tag in front (the day it was booked for, or how long it has waited); **a grey scroll bar under the calls when they do not all fit** (`overflow-x: auto`, 12px). The amber and red frames are gone. A click on the name box still closes and opens the line. Looked at in a test picture beside a No tech yet line (the scroll bar itself does not draw in the test browser). **Not seen on the live app.**
- **Not looked into yet**: George wrote "throughout the entire dispatch board". Other ways the two boards can differ (a call moved to another tech or time in ServiceTitan between two syncs; how often the sync runs) were not checked; ask George for another example if he sees one after this.

## Dispatch ✨ Optimize

- Flags calls outside the tech's area for the day, then suggests a move to a tech with room in that area, or, if nobody has room, a swap of two calls between two techs (ticked/unticked and undone together). Nothing changes until Carlo approves.
- Never swap a call whose tech already has its parts in his truck (`dispatch_parts_held`, `sync/extra/218_parts_on_truck.sql`: a received PO he ordered for the job, or stock received/transferred into his truck for the job). Carlo's rule, Nov 7, 2026.
- Clicking a call on the board shows its own Optimize suggestion in the details panel ("Move it to Scott" / "Make this swap"), which makes just that one change after a yes; Undo puts it back.

## Contact tech / Ask the office to call me

- Blue 📞 button in the left side bar. Office/owners: "Contact tech" (pick a tech) → his screen shows "Call Carlo at the office". Techs: "Ask the office to call me" (pick an office person or owner) → their screen shows "Nick wants you to call him" with his phone if we have it. Optional "What about?" note.
- The pop-up sits at the top of the screen (beeps, vibrates on phones) with a ⏱ timer of how long it's been waiting (yellow at 10 min, red at 30). Got it = accepted (logged); it stays until they press Call back done, then the sender gets a green "Nick called you back" pop-up (OK closes it). 📋 Log in the Contact dialog: sent / Got it / called back times (owners see everyone's). `220_call_requests_log.sql`. `sync/extra/219_call_requests.sql`, `startCallReq` / `openContact` in `index.html`.

## Book a call (office)

- One question at a time (`bkWizard`): Reid or a Partner (→ pick the partner) → kind of call (Service, Maintenance, Warranty, Estimate, Install…) → which job type. The department fills in from the job type (`unit_id`), then the rest of the booking shows with "Reid › Service › Service Call - Regular Hours · Department" crumbs to change any step. A partner sees its own job types first ("Vista - Maintenance"); other partners' types are hidden. Vista asks who pays for the tank only for installs / water heaters. "Another visit" (open job) is on the first step. The partner step shows app squares with each partner's logo big (the Partners page logos, `partner_logos_list` / `PP_LOGOS`, else the invoice branding logo; coloured initials until one is added).
- Picking the tech: every office booking (new call or another visit) uses the same Best techs to send squares + Calls nearby board as the customer page (`bkNearHtml`, `nearCallsHtml(..., { pick: true, bkAll: true })`). The tech's kind comes from the job type. Pick fills the tech, day and window, so the Day and Arrival window rows are gone there (`bkNearOnly`); "Pick by hand" brings back the chips, and "Not booked yet" is in the Picked line.
- Another visit: a 4th purple box "Last tech there" (`last_tech_there`, `sync/extra/222_last_tech.sql`) opens his next 10 working days to pick from. Picking the job fills "This visit is for" (`Another call for Job …`) and starts the notes with "This is another visit on open job …" (`bkVisitFill`).
- Call Back (Reid → Service): opens "which call is it for?" (the customer's earlier jobs with a visit: type, job number, last date and tech; `callback_jobs`, `sync/extra/228_call_backs.sql`). The new Call Back job is attached to it (`jobs.original_job_id` via `set_job_original`), the summary/notes start with "Call back for Job …", and the Pick the tech box shows the purple Last tech there square (open) for that job. Open, it shows everyone's next 10 working days (techs of the right kind, the last tech first in purple; free windows are green, click one to pick that tech and day).
- Install (Reid): first asks "book a sold estimate, or start from scratch?": the customer's sold (accepted) estimates with the sold option; picking one books a visit on its install job (`est_install_job`, made with `est_ensure_install_job` if missing), installers only, summary/notes "Install: … (estimate …)"; Start from scratch (BO 4) shows only Gas Piping and 📖 Price Book: search the price book, pick items; the equipment count decides Install x 1–3, the length is the items' labour hours, summary/notes list the items (`bkwPbRes`, `BKW_IT`).
- "🗓 Go to the dispatch board and pick by hand": the board waits for a click on a free spot (any day, any tech), asks "Book the call for this day and tech?" (Accept / Cancel), books it with everything from the form (`bkBoardBook` → `bkDoBook`), then opens the customer page with a ✓ Booked message.

- **Book a call: the priority is one plain word in the top right corner of BO 1** (Carlo, Nov 11, 4:17 p.m.: "remove Priority · Normal · Set by the kind of call and move it to the top right corner of BO 1 and only have it say what it is, example Normal"). The Priority row is gone from the booking form. The word **Normal**, **High** or **Emergency** (white, bold, no box around it; Emergency in yellow) sits in the top right corner of the blue banner at the top of the page (`.bk-prio-corner`, written inside the `h1`, placed by CSS on `.book .cl-hero`). **I got this wrong twice and he was upset** ("you are not paying attention… I want it in the top right corner like I have told you and do not make it a button just make it a word", 4:22 p.m.): first I put a chip in the corner of the form (that is BO 2); then inside the `h1` with CSS for `.book > h1`, which never matched. **Why: `pageBanners` (near the end of `index.html`) moves every staff page's `h1` and its `p.lead` into a banner, `header.cl-hero.auto-hero`, after the page is drawn. So BO 1 on such a page is that banner, the `h1` is not a direct child of the `article` on the screen, and CSS written for `article > h1` does nothing.** Before placing anything "in XX 1", draw the page top in a test picture with the banner (scratch `bo.js`). Every new call the office books has the word; not on another visit and not on a sales call. Looked at in a test picture with the app's styles. **Not seen on the live app.**

The old small row "1 Add the work · 2 Finish · 3 Customer signs · 4 Payment" is hidden while a not-finished invoice is being worked on, so there are not two sets of numbers; it still shows on a finished invoice, where Customer signature and Take the payment follow as before.
