## Vista install: who pays for the tank → invoice → Tank P.O. → Credit Order (Nov 11)

- (Carlo, Nov 11, 11:20 a.m.: customer → Book a call → A Partner → Vista → Install: "it should have a pop up asking who is paying for the tank, Us or Vista, then it should open an invoice up and ask us to pick from the price book to add an item. At the bottom of the invoice it should have a created P.O.… if Vista pays… then a Credit Order should be created that waits for the credit to come back from the supplier".)
- **The pop-up**: as soon as a Vista job that needs a tank is picked in the Book a call questions (`bkNeedTank`: an install, or a water heater / tank job type), a box in the middle of the screen asks **"Who is paying for the tank?"** with two big choices, **Us: Reid pays for the tank** / **Vista pays for the tank** (`bkTankAsk`, called from `bkwPick`; it sets `F.tank`). The "Who pays for the tank?" row on the booking is still there to change it; closing the box without picking leaves that row to pick from, and the booking still will not save without it.
- **After the call is booked** (the Book button, or a spot picked on the dispatch board): the job's invoice is started and opened right away (`bkTankInvoice` → `new_invoice` → `openInvoiceEditor`, message "✓ The call is booked (job …). Now pick the tank from the price book."), instead of the usual booked screen. The price book search has a green frame, "👉 Pick the tank from the price book. Type its name here, then click it.", and the cursor is in it (`.ie-find-tank`, `IE_TANK_GO`). If the invoice can not be started, it says so and shows the usual booked screen.
- **Bottom of the invoice: 📦 Tank P.O.** (`ieTankPoHtml`, `ieTankPoWire`, `.ie-tankpo`; shown on any invoice of a job whose `tank_payer` is `reid` or `partner`; data from RPC `tank_order_for`, loaded in `openInvoiceEditor` as `I.tankpo`). No tank on the invoice yet: "I can not make the P.O. yet. First add the tank…". With lines: **1 Which line is the tank?** (the price book lines of the invoice; the one the partner rule calls the tank, or an equipment line, is picked already) · **2 Which supplier did we buy the tank from?** (the suppliers list, or Someone else…) · a green **Create the P.O.** (we pay) or **Create the P.O. and the Credit Order** (Vista pays). RPC `tank_order_make(p_invoice, p_line, p_supplier, p_supplier_name)`: makes a job PO with the usual `get_po_number` (so the number is job number-1, a job note is written, and it shows in the customer page's Attached column), adds the tank as its line (the invoice line's item, quantity and cost), and keeps the tie in table **`tank_orders`** (invoice, invoice line, job, supplier, PO, who pays). After: "✔ P.O. 1234-1 · bought from Boone" with **Open the P.O.**
- **Credit Order (Vista pays)**: the same row gets a credit order number **CO-<id>**, status `waiting`, amount = the tank's cost, and a job note. On the invoice: a yellow "⏳ Credit Order CO-7: waiting for the credit from Boone" with a green **✓ The credit came back** (asks for the supplier's credit number and the amount; office people and owners: `_tank_office`; RPC `tank_credit_set(id, 'received' | 'waiting' | 'cancel', ref, amount)`), then "✔ … the credit came back" with Undo. **Tasks page**: "⏳ Credit orders waiting on a supplier" (office and owners; `tankCreditBox`, RPC `tank_credits_waiting`): one line each with the supplier, the amount, links to the customer, the invoice and the P.O., and the same button.
- **"Which supplier did we buy the tank from?" only lists the suppliers we buy it from** (Carlo, Nov 13, 8:53 a.m.: "only list supplier that we buy them from, not all of them"). `ieTankSupOpts(I, line, all)`: the drop-down shows the suppliers of the picked tank line's own item (its supplier part numbers in `supplier_items`, and its usual supplier, `preferred_supplier_id`); when the item has none, every supplier we buy tanks from (ones on a Tank P.O. or a Vista tank payment before, or that carry an equipment item named water heater / hot water / tankless / tank). When only one fits it is picked already. The last choice is **Another supplier…**, which opens the whole list with Someone else…, so nobody is stuck. Picking another tank line redraws the list for that line. RPC `tank_suppliers_for(p_invoice)` → `{by_item, tanks}` (`sync/extra/274_tank_suppliers.sql`; kept in `I.tanksups`). **If the look-up fails or finds nothing, the whole list shows as before.** **The database was not reachable from the chat on Nov 13, so the SQL was not run by me: it goes live with the push (the usual few minutes), and I could not see which suppliers it gives. Check with him; if a tank's supplier is missing, add that supplier on the tank's price book page (Who do we buy it from?).** Checked: the script parses.
- Database: `sync/extra/259_tank_orders.sql` (live Nov 11). Costs in these answers are only sent to people who see costs.
- **What it does not do (tell him if he asks)**: the credit order is only a record that waits; it is not tied to the supplier's credit paper when that is loaded on Supplier invoices (someone presses "The credit came back"), and it does not change Our statement or the job costing. It does not touch the older "Who pays for the tank" box on the invoice (`inv_set_supplier_pay`) or the Vista tank pairs. Only Vista (the partner whose name has "vista"), as the booking code already did. A job booked before today with a tank answer also shows the Tank P.O. section when its invoice is opened.
- Checked: the script parses; the database functions were made and `tank_credits_waiting` answers; the Tank P.O. section was built from the real code with pretend data (no tank yet, the form, waiting, credit back) and looked at in a test picture. **The pop-up, the booking → invoice step, making a real P.O. and the Tasks box were not run, and nothing was seen on the live app.**

## Supplier credits: Credit Memos, and Credit Orders that answer themselves (Nov 11)

- (Carlo, Nov 11, 4:05 p.m.: "I want the credit order to be automated like all the rest of the packing slips and invoices… anything from a supplier invoice that came back as a credit should have the option of now creating a Credit Memo, and any in the past that we made a P.O. will need to be fixed".) Database: `sync/extra/260_credit_memos.sql` (live Nov 11), table **`supplier_credit_memos`** (CM-<id>: the supplier, the credit paper, the P.O. it is against when known, the credit order it answered, the P.O. it replaced, how: `hand` / `auto` / `fixed`).
- **A Credit Order answers itself.** When a supplier's credit paper lands in Supplier invoices (from the mailbox, a drop-in, a statement: any way; trigger `trg_credit_order_auto` on `supplier_invoices` → `_credit_order_auto`), and a Credit Order is waiting for that supplier with the tank's P.O. number on the paper (in the PO text, the original order or the job name, or the paper is matched to that P.O.), the app marks the Credit Order "the credit came back" with the paper's number and amount, makes the Credit Memo, and writes a job note. Nobody presses anything. The paper can also come first (`trg_tank_order_look`). An Undo on the credit order stays undone (the same paper does not match it again). The button "✓ The credit came back" is still there for a credit that does not carry the P.O. number. The waiting line on the invoice says "When their credit paper comes in with P.O. … on it, I mark this by myself."; a matched one says "I matched their credit paper by myself" (`credit_auto`).
- **A Credit Memo app box on every supplier credit** (an open paper in Supplier invoices whose kind is credit or return, or whose total is below zero; `sinvMemoTile`, `sinvIsCredit`, `d.memo` from RPC `credit_memo_for`): blue **Create a Credit Memo** (asks first; RPC `credit_memo_make`), then green **Credit Memo made · CM-12** (a click shows the amount, the P.O., the credit order, who made it), with "Not a credit? Take it off" (`credit_memo_remove`; not when it answered a credit order). It is an option: credits do not get a memo by themselves, except when they answer a Credit Order.
- **A credit no longer gets a P.O.** The "here's a PO made from it" box (`sinvMakePoHtml`) is not offered on a credit or a return: it says "This is a credit. A credit does not get a P.O. Press the blue Create a Credit Memo box at the top." `supplier_invoice_make_po` itself is unchanged (invoices still use it).
- **The past was fixed (11 credits)**: every credit that had a P.O. made from it (the S-10xx POs with "from credit" in their description, made Oct 30 to Nov 11, none on a job, none with another paper on it) now has a Credit Memo (`how = 'fixed'`), the P.O. was taken off the credit, and the P.O. is **cancelled, not deleted** (its note says why; `replaced_po_id` keeps which one). Making a memo by hand on such a credit does the same. The block is in the file, so any that slip through later are fixed on the next database update. To put one back: set the PO's status back and `supplier_invoices.purchase_order_id`, and delete the memo row.
- **A tag on the Supplier invoices list, and every older credit has a memo** (Carlo, Nov 11, 4:13 p.m.: "make it a tag on the Supplier invoices list. yes give the 1233 older credits a memo now"). Each credit's line in the list shows a green tag "🧾 Credit Memo CM-12" beside its number (`SINV.memos` from RPC `credit_memos_list(p_supplier)`, loaded with the list; `.sinv-memo-tag`). All 1,234 credits that had no memo got one straight in the database on Nov 11 (`how = 'old'`; a one-time run, not in the file), so 1,245 credits have a memo and none is left without. **Credits that come in from now on still get a memo only from the box, or by themselves when they answer a Credit Order**; returns were not given one.
- **Credit Memos has its own tab, like Purchase orders** (Carlo, Nov 11, 4:18 p.m.: "there should be a tab for Credit Memos like there is for Purchase Orders"). Side bar button **Credit Memos** right after POs (`#cmemo-link`, page key `cmemos`, `#cmemos`, in `PAGE_GROUPS` with Stock / Purchase orders / Supplier invoices; shown to the people who see POs, less the warehouse because it shows money). `openCreditMemos`, `loadCreditMemos`, `renderCreditMemos`, `CMP`, `CMEMO_KINDS` (not `CM_KINDS`: that name is Marketing's). The page: 1 Find a Credit Memo (memo number, the supplier's credit number, a P.O., a supplier) · 2 Which supplier? · 3 Which ones? (All of them / Answered a Credit Order / Replaced a P.O. / Made since Nov 11) · the "You are looking at" bar with the count and the dollars · a list (memo, date of the credit, supplier, their credit number, amount, the P.O. it is for, what it did), the newest 300. A click on a line opens the supplier's credit paper in Supplier invoices; the P.O. number opens the P.O. RPC `credit_memo_list(p_search, p_supplier, p_kind, p_limit)` (in 260, live). There is no "new credit memo" button on the page: a memo is made from the credit paper. Checked: the script parses. **The page was not run or looked at in a picture, and the list function could only be run without a signed-in person (it answers empty then), so its real answer was not seen.**
- **Credit Memos show the customer they belong to, and Vista's can be picked** (Carlo, Nov 12, 8:40 p.m.: "can you match the credit memos to the Vista customers, most of them belong to them; we are going to create an invoice system soon that helps us keep track of this better than Titan did"). The Credit Memos page has a column **Customer it belongs to** (the customer's name, a click opens their page; a green "Vista" tag; "Our invoice 1234" under it) and three new choices in 3 Which ones?: **Vista customers · Other customers · No customer found yet**; the search box also finds a customer's name. `credit_memo_list` (in `sync/extra/260_credit_memos.sql`, live Nov 12) finds the customer through our invoice attached to the credit paper (`supplier_invoice_customer_links`; a Vista one first: the invoice is billed to the partner whose name has "vista", or its job is that partner's); new kinds `vista`, `cust`, `nocust`. **What the data gave on Nov 12, of 1,250 credit memos: 388 belong to a Vista customer ($568,476.22), 129 to another customer, 733 have no customer yet** (396 of those are Lennox, 110 Boone, 71 Trent main, 53 Trent water heaters, 51 Master, 44 ADI). **Nothing new was tied by me: I looked for more ties and found no safe ones.** Tried: the credit's P.O. to a job (none left that were not already tied), the number printed on the credit against job numbers, ServiceTitan numbers and partner reference numbers (0), another paper with the same printed number that is tied to a customer (2). **A guess I did not save, waiting on his yes**: 95 credits have exactly one invoice of the same supplier for the same amount in the 90 days before, and that invoice is tied to a customer (14 of them Vista); tank prices repeat, so for most credits more than one invoice fits. Checked: the script parses; the function was made and its three counts were read with plain SQL. **The page was not run or looked at in a picture, and not seen on the live app.**
- **🔄 Search again on the Credit Memos page** (Carlo, Nov 12, 8:56 p.m.: "will the cmemos page always look for this, or do you need to add a search again button that is easy to find and see and is square and matches the page"). The page already looks each time it is opened: the customer is read fresh from the ties (`supplier_invoice_customer_links`), and those ties are made by a person on the credit paper (Find our invoice) or by the app itself when a P.O. or job changes and at each sync (`_sinv_autolink`). The square (`#cm-again`, 104px, soft look, top right of the choices) loads the list again without leaving the page and then says "✓ I looked again at 8:58 p.m." (`CMP.again`). It does not run a new match by itself. Checked: the script parses. **Not looked at in a picture and not seen on the live app.**
- **What it does not do**: a Credit Memo is a record on the credit paper; it does not change Our statement, the supplier's balance or job costing (the credit paper itself already counts there).
- Checked: the script parses; on the live database, in a test that was rolled back: a credit paper with the tank's P.O. number marked a waiting credit order received and made the memo, an undone one stayed undone, and a credit for something else did not match; the 11 past ones were counted after the fix (11 memos, 11 POs cancelled). **The Credit Memo box and its clicks were not run or looked at in a picture, and nothing was seen on the live app.**

## HSG Pro logo (left side bar)

- Only in the left side bar now (`.sl-badge#hsg-badge`, Reid logo + HSG Pro); the one at the bottom of the Customers page was taken off (Carlo, Nov 9). Clicking it pops a message bubble beside it.

- **Every 8th click on the logo, Eco Man dances** (it was every 5th; Carlo, Nov 10, 7:48 p.m.: "when I hit the logo button 8 times a dancing Eco Man appears, but use the 3D one with no background that I added"). **The 3D Eco Man is in** (he dragged `strong-house-3d.zip` into the chat at 7:56 p.m.: five faces, each as a picture with a background, a see-through picture, a moving gif and an mp4; the moving ones have a pale blue background, so "no background" means the see-through still picture). The five see-through ones are in the repo as `eco/eco-3d-<face>.webp` (happy, sad, confused, angry, impatient; 640px, cut close, about 40 KB each). The logo dance passes `img: "eco/eco-3d-happy.webp"` to `ecoDance`, which then gives the picture class `eco-dance-3d` (he bounces and sways, `@keyframes ecoDance3d`, since the picture itself does not move). **Every Eco Man is the 3D one now** (Carlo, Nov 10, 7:58 p.m.: "yes change everything to the 3D"): `ECO_3D(face)` gives the picture's address; `ECO_FACES` / `ecoFace()` / `ECO_MAN_SVG` are now `<img class="eco-man">` tags (the old inline drawings, about 37 KB, are gone from `index.html`), and the pop-ups (`ecoPop`, the red and violet ones too), the thinking box (`ecoThink`) and every dance (`ecoDance`) use them. The pictures are still, so a pop-up's Eco Man bobs a little (`@keyframes ecoBob`) and the dancer bounces and sways. Where the notes below say "inline SVG" or `eco/strong-house-<face>.gif`, read "the 3D pictures"; the old gifs are still in `eco/` but nothing uses them. The folder called **deposit photos** (paid stock photos, to make the site friendlier) has still not been seen: no folder is connected to the chat. He dances in the middle of the screen for 5 seconds ("That is 8 clicks, Carlo! Here is a little dance for you."), instead of a message (Carlo, Nov 10). It is for fun: not saved, the boss is not told (`ecoDance(place, { fun: true, secs, lines })`, `HSG.clicks` in `hsgSay`).
- A click on the logo shows a message; **a second click turns it off**, the next click shows a new one, and so on (Carlo, Nov 10; the check at the top of `hsgSay`). Left alone, a message still goes away by itself after 9 seconds.
- The side bar badge has a "👆 Click me" tag; clicking it shows one of 200 kind messages (`HSG_MSGS`), going through all 200 before any repeats (kept in localStorage), some using how long they've been with us from the Staff page "Started working here" date (`sync/extra/221_staff_started.sql`).
- Service → "Service Call" is one button, then "Regular hours or after hours?" (`BKW_SC`). Priority is never picked by hand: after hours = Emergency, everything else Normal. After hours books only the on-call tech (picked automatically) or the 2nd on call (`bkAHBox`), today from now for 3 hours (before 7 am it's last night's on call); no other tech list. Reid's buttons: Non Revenue (pick up parts types), no Phone Call Request anywhere on Book a call (Carlo, Nov 8), Reid's Warranty - HVAC under Service as "Warranty Call" (asks regular or after hours like Service Call; after hours = same on-call/emergency rules, `F.ah`, and the job summary starts "AFTER HOURS –" so the invoice is marked) and Warranty - Security under Security, no Humidifier button for Reid, HVAC Quote's button says "Book a Sales Consultant" and Walk SIte for Job's says "Book Tech to Walk Site for Job" and only installers are offered for it (`bkKindOf`) (`bkwLabel`; the job type keeps its name), Gas Piping and Hot Water Tank under Install (`bkwCatOf`), Maintenance 4 (`sync/extra/225_maintenance_4.sql`). Reid → Maintenance opens the maintenance options straight away (Furnace Cleaning, Air Conditioner Cleaning… `maint_options`, `227_maint_options.sql`, "Change this list"); how many are picked decides the job type: 1 = Maintenance 1 … 4 = Maintenance 4 (no more than 4). The picks become "What's wrong / what's needed". The ⏱ time given for Maintenance 1–4 is set on that page (owners/dispatch; `job_type_minutes`, `226_job_type_minutes.sql`, applied in `bookLists`) and is the booking length on the dispatch board and online booking. An after-hours call is clearly marked: orange AFTER HOURS CALL banner on the booking and on the invoice editor (orange header), and an "After-hours service call" tag + orange top on the invoice paper.

## Online booking from marketing emails

- `{book_link}` fill-in (and the "Book your tune-up online" template) gives each customer their own link (`?book=<marketing_token>`) to a public calendar page (`showOnlineBook`, no sign-in). It offers the best arrival windows (8–11, 11–2, 2–5) from 14 days out to 60 days (Marketing → Email → Online booking settings): free techs of the right kind (maintenance or service techs), minus calls with no tech yet in that window, plus a bonus for a call within 5 km that day; at most 2 per day, 12 offers. None work → "please call the office" with the phone. The booking makes the job + visit with no tech (assign on Dispatch) and a job note. `sync/extra/223_online_booking.sql` (`public_book_info`, `public_book`; `p_dry` checks it and rolls back).

## On call (dispatch board)

- Who is on call each day: `on_call` table (`sync/extra/224_on_call.sql`), loaded with Nov–Dec 2026 from Carlo's Shifter calendar screenshots (top name = on call, bottom name, Adam or Dave = 2nd on call). Seed rows only fill empty days, so changes made in the app stay. Day view shows an orange "📟 On call … · 2nd: …" strip above Waiting for a day (Change = pick from drop-downs, owners/dispatch) and a 📟 tag on those people's lines. January isn't in yet. **The on-call tech's whole box on his line is orange** (Carlo, Nov 12, 4:54 p.m.: "have the whole box orange if they are on call, not just a small spot"): the box with his picture and name (`.tl-row > header:has(.oc-tag)`) is orange (#e8590c) with white words, the 2nd on call a lighter orange (#fd7e14); the small tag in it is white with orange words. CSS only. **Not looked at in a picture and not seen on the live app.**

## Eco Man (the mascot)

- **Settings → Eco Man tab: all his pictures, and which one is used where** (Carlo, Nov 10, 8:34 p.m.: "make a tab in settings for eco man and have all of them posted there so i can pick them and use them as I want for places"). Owners only. Settings now has two tabs at the top, **⚙ Settings** and **🏠 Eco Man** (`.set-tabs`, `data-set-tab`, class `set-eco-on` on the article; `#eco-set`, `ecoSetBox`, `ECO_SET`). The tab shows **1 Pick a place** (nine squares, each with the picture used there now) and **2 the pictures** (ten squares, tagged "He moves" or "Still picture", and how many places use each). Pick a place, click a picture: it is saved at once, for everyone (`set_eco_pic(place, file)`; null = the usual one back; `eco_pics()` reads; `company_settings.eco_pics`; `sync/extra/256_eco_pics.sql`, live). Others get it within 10 minutes or after a refresh (`ecoPicksLoad`, kept in localStorage `reid.eco.picks`).
  - **🏠 Eco Man button at the top of every page, owners only** (Carlo, Nov 10, 9:23 p.m.: "add an eco man setting button at the top of all of Carlo's pages; when I press it it shows where he is on the page, and if I click him he goes to the options page with him and lets me change him"). `#eco-find-btn` in the top bar beside Page settings (`ecoFindSet`, `ECO_FIND`): every Eco Man picture showing on the page gets a pulsing violet frame (`img.eco-found`) and a violet bar under the top says "Eco Man is in 3 places on this page… Click him to change him", with **Open his settings** and **Close**. A click on a framed Eco Man opens Settings → Eco Man with **that place already picked** (`ecoFindGo`, `ecoPlaceOf`: the picture's `data-eco-place`, else the place that uses that file), so the next click on a picture changes him. When he is not on the page (he mostly pops up), the bar says so and still offers his settings. While it is on, a click on a framed Eco Man does not make him dance. **New Eco Man pictures should carry `data-eco-place="<key>"`.** I took "Carlo's pages" to mean every page when an owner is signed in.
  - **➕ Add him to this page** (Carlo, Nov 10, 9:40 p.m.: "when I click the eco man button have a button open as an option to add him to a page; when I click on a location it lets me choose the eco man I want to use"). The violet bar of the 🏠 Eco Man button has **➕ Add him to this page**: the bar then says "Click the spot on this page where you want Eco Man" (the mouse is a cross; a click on a button does not press it), a violet dot marks the spot, and a box asks **1 How big?** (Small / Medium / Large) and **2 which picture** (all of `ECO_LIB`); one click on a picture puts him there, for everyone. With the bar open, a click on an Eco Man that was put on by hand opens the same box to change his picture or size, or **🗑 Take him off this page** (a second click to be sure). Owners only add, change and take off; everyone sees him; a normal click still makes him dance. He does not print.
    - How: `company_settings.eco_spots` = a list of `{id, page, sel, fx, fy, file, size}` (`sync/extra/257_eco_spots.sql`, live: `eco_spots()`, `eco_spot_set(p)`, `eco_spot_del(p_id)`; at most 60). `page` = the app's page key (`body[data-page]`, "search" for the first page; so one put on a customer's page shows on every customer's page). `sel` = "M …" or "S …": a path from `#main` or `#search-page` to the piece of the page that was clicked (`ecoSelOf`: tag, two plain classes, which one of its kind; it stops at a piece with its own id); `fx`, `fy` = where in that piece, in percent. `ecoSpotsApply` draws him (`span.eco-spot > img.eco-man[data-eco-spot]`, the piece gets `eco-spot-host`), and a watcher (`ecoSpotsWatch`) puts him back 0.3 s after the page is drawn again. Loaded with the picks (`ecoSpotsLoad`, kept in localStorage `reid.eco.spots`). `ecoSpotClick`, `ecoSpotPick`, `#eco-pick`, `ECO_FIND.placing`.
    - **Limits to tell him if he asks**: he is tied to a piece of the page, not to a customer or a line of data: on a list he sits on "the third line", whatever is on it; if a page's layout is changed later, he may move or not show (then put him on again); a piece that cuts off what sticks out of it will cut him off; he can cover a button under him.
    - Checked: the script parses; the real code was run in a test browser on a pretend page with a pretend database: add (on plain words and in a table line), the box, redraw of the page (he comes back), another page (he is not there), change size, take off. **Not seen on the live app.**
  - **A red 🗑 Delete tag on every Eco Man while the bar is open** (Carlo, Nov 11, 4:59 p.m.: "when an eco man is on the page and I open the Eco Man setting button have a way for me to click on one and delete it from the page without leaving the page"). With the 🏠 Eco Man bar open, each Eco Man on the page has a red tag at his top right corner (`.eco-del-x`, `ecoDelTags`; it follows him when the page scrolls). One click: "Sure? Click again" (5 seconds); the second click takes him off, for everyone, and the page stays where it is ("✓ He is off this page." in the bar). One put on by hand is deleted (`eco_spot_del`). **One that is part of the page itself** (a click on him used to go to Settings) is hidden: `company_settings.eco_hidden` = `[{id, page, sel}]`, the path to the piece he sits in (`ecoHidSel`: his parent), and his pictures in that piece get class `eco-hid` (`ECO_HID`, `ecoHidApply`, called from `ecoSpotsApply`, so he stays off after the page is drawn again; localStorage `reid.eco.hid`; RPCs `eco_hidden()`, `eco_hide_add(p)`, `eco_hide_clear(p_page)`, owners only; `sync/extra/262_eco_hidden.sql`, live Nov 11). The bar then has **↩ Put back the 2 I took off** (all the hidden ones of that page come back). Only his picture is hidden: his word bubble, if he has one, stays. No tag on a pop-up or the dancer (they are not in the page and go away by themselves). Like a spot, it goes by the piece of the page, so on a customer's page it is every customer's page, and a layout change later can bring him back. Checked in a test browser with the real code (one of each kind taken off, put back). **Not seen on the live app.**
  - **🖼 Change him on the logo · 🔄 Change him on the refresh box** (Carlo, Nov 10, 9:49 p.m.: "have a way for me to change the eco man on the logo and refresh box as well"). Two more buttons in the violet bar of the 🏠 Eco Man button. He is not on the page in those two places until they happen (8 clicks on the logo; a new update), so there is nothing to click: each button opens the picture box for that place (`ecoPlacePick("logo" | "update")`, the box `#eco-pick`), the one used now has a green frame, and one click on a picture saves it for everyone (`set_eco_pic`, the same save as Settings → Eco Man; no database change). Checked in a test browser with a pretend database. **Not seen on the live app.**
  - **Anyone can drag him** (Carlo, Nov 10, 10:14 p.m.: "when I click on an eco man that is dancing or moving but not giving instructions I want to be able to move him around as a user, drag him up down right or left"). Hold the mouse (or a finger) on him and pull: an Eco Man put on a page by hand, the dancer (the boss dance and the logo dance) and the thinking one. **Not** the ones that talk in a bubble (pop-ups, the papers) and not the one on the green refresh box: those give instructions, and the green box is one big button. It moves him on that person's screen only (`ecoDragWire`, CSS `translate` on the picture, so his dance keeps going; class `eco-dragging`). One put on a page by hand stays where that person dragged him, on that computer (localStorage `reid.eco.drag`, by spot id); it does not move him for other people (an owner who wants that takes him off and adds him again). A short click with no pull is still a click (he dances; the click that ends a pull is swallowed, `ECO_FIND.dragAt`); off while the 🏠 Eco Man bar is open. Checked in a test browser (drag, the page drawn again). **Not seen on the live app, and not tried with a finger on a phone.**
  - **The bump rule: two Eco Men never sit on top of each other** (Carlo, Nov 10, 10:23 p.m.: "if 2 images walk or bump or walk into each other have them move the other way, even if it's because the user moved them on purpose"). When one is dragged into another, the one that is bumped moves away, straight out from the middle of the one coming at him, and keeps getting pushed while the drag goes on. If the bumped one has no room left on the screen, the dragged one bounces back when it is let go (`img.eco-bumped`, a short springy move). Two put on a page on the same spot are moved apart 0.7 s after the page is drawn (`window.ecoBumpAll`). It works between the ones that can be dragged (put on a page by hand, the dancer, the thinking one); the ones in word bubbles do not move. The pictures do not walk across the page by themselves (a "walking" picture walks inside its own frame), so a bump only comes from a drag or from two put on the same spot. Each person's pushed spots are kept on their computer like a drag (`reid.eco.drag`). In `ecoDragWire`: `box` (the picture's frame less 15% on each side, so the see-through edge does not count), `hit`, `apart`. A bumped one that lands on a third one pushes him too, and so on (`shove`). Checked in a test browser with two on a pretend page. **Not seen on the live app.**
  - **The wall rule: he can not go onto a thing on the screen** (Carlo, Nov 10, 10:50 p.m.: "if an image hits a box or anything on the screen have it move the other way"). While he is dragged, or pushed by another Eco Man, he is stopped by: a button, a typing box, a drop-down, a link, a picture, a heading, and any box with a frame around it (a border on its top and left). He is put back out on the side he came in from, 12px away from it, so he slides along things and through the gaps between them; he can not leave the screen. **Not walls**: the boxes he sits inside; anything he is already on top of when the drag starts (so one put on a button on purpose can still be pulled off it, and then can not go back on); boxes over a third of the screen (or the whole page would be one wall). An Eco Man who is pushed and has a wall behind him stays, and the dragged one is sent back. It is only checked while something moves him: one put on a button with ➕ Add him stays there until someone drags him. The dancer and the thinking one are only stopped by things inside their own box, and by the screen's edge. In `ecoDragWire`: `wallsRaw` (the list of things, made once when a drag starts and again if the page scrolls), `wallsFor`, `onWall`, `solid`, `d.good` (the last free spot, where he goes back to if he is stuck). Checked in a test browser: dragged onto a button (kept off), off the screen's edge (kept in), open space (moves freely), one pushing another. **Not seen on the live app. On a page full of boxes (a list, a table with lines) he will have little room to move: if Carlo finds that too stiff, make fewer things walls (`WALL_TAG`, `framed`).**
  - **Stretch him and pop him off the screen for 10 minutes** (Carlo, Nov 10, 10:53 p.m.: "if you click and hold an eco man and drag the mouse have it stretch him and when let go have it slam back into him and pop him off the screen for 10 minutes"). Both this and the drag rule start with a pull, so they are told apart by the **hold**: a quick pull moves him (as before); **hold the mouse down on him without moving for about half a second** and he glows yellow (`img.eco-held`), and a pull then **stretches** him toward the mouse like rubber (CSS `scale`, up to 3 times, the far side stays put). Let go: he snaps back with a bounce, then flies off the screen the opposite way, spinning (`popOff`, the Web Animations API), and stays away **10 minutes on that person's screen only** (`ECO_GONE`, localStorage `reid.eco.gone`, by spot id; `ecoSpotsApply` leaves those out and `ecoGoneBack` brings him back when the time is up; a refresh does not bring him back early). Same Eco Men as the drag rule (put on a page by hand, the dancer, the thinking one: those two are just hidden, they go away by themselves anyway). Hold and let go without pulling = nothing (he dances, as a click). Checked in a test browser: quick pull moves, hold then pull stretches, let go and he is gone, the page drawn again and he stays gone, back after the time. **Not seen on the live app, and not tried with a finger on a phone. If Carlo meant every pull should stretch (no moving him any more), take out the 450 ms hold.**
  - **The stretch is like an elastic** (Carlo, Nov 10, 10:59 p.m.: "make him stretch more like an elastic would"). It used to make him wider and taller, straight across and straight down. Now he is pulled **along the line of the pull, at any angle**, he gets **thinner across it as he gets longer**, and he **pulls back harder the farther he goes** (the stretch slows toward about 3.4 times; `k = 1 + 2.4 × (1 − e^(−pull ÷ 1.5 sizes))`, across = `1/√k`). The side away from the mouse stays pinned (`transform-origin`). Let go: he shoots back past his own shape and **wobbles, each swing smaller** (five steps in 0.46 s, a bigger first swing after a longer pull), then flies off as before. It is a CSS `transform` (`stretchT(angle, k)` = rotate, scale, rotate back) on the picture, so his own dance is stopped while he is held (`animation: none`). Where the note above says CSS `scale`, read this. Checked in a test browser (a slanted pull: longer along it, thinner across; let go and he is gone). **Not seen on the live app.**
  - **A slider for his size** (Carlo, Nov 10, 11:02 p.m.: "for the eco man add a sliding scale I can also use to change his size"). In the box that adds or changes an Eco Man on a page (`ecoSpotPick`), under Small / Medium / Large: "Or slide to any size: Small ▬▬●▬▬ Big" with the number beside it (his height in pixels, 40 to 400, steps of 5; `.ep-range`, `.ep-px`). For one already on the page, the box goes see-through while the slider moves (`#eco-pick.ep-peek`) so he is seen growing and shrinking on the page, and it is saved for everyone when the slider is let go. For a new one, set the slider, then click the picture. The size is kept as `s`, `m`, `l` or the number (`ecoSpotPx`; `eco_spot_set` takes a number from 40 to 400 now: `sync/extra/257_eco_spots.sql`, patched live). Owners only, like the rest of that box. Only for the ones put on a page by hand: the pop-ups, the dancer and the refresh box have their own fixed sizes. **Also: the violet bar of the 🏠 Eco Man button is at the bottom of the screen now** (it was under the top bar): with five buttons it had grown tall enough to cover an Eco Man near the top of a page, so he could not be clicked. Checked in a test browser (slide to 260, he is 260 on the page, saved, still 260 after the page is drawn again). **Not seen on the live app.**
  - **He stretches up to about 10 times now** (Carlo, Nov 10, 11:06 p.m.: "have the eco man stretch even more, up to 3 times as much as now"; the top was about 3.4 times). `k = 1 + 9.2 × (1 − e^(−pull ÷ 4.5 sizes))`: the first part of the pull feels about the same as before, and a pull across the whole screen takes him to 8 or 9 times as long and about a third as thick. Where the notes above say 3.4 times, read 10. Checked in a test browser. **Not seen on the live app.**
  - **Thinner when stretched, and he pops when let go** (Carlo, Nov 10, 11:16 p.m.: "when I click hold and pull him I want it to get thinner than it does, more like an elastic would, and I want it to pop and disappear when it's let go"). Across the pull he is now `k^-0.8` as thick (it was `1/√k`): at 3 times as long he is about 0.4 as thick (was 0.58), at 8 times about 0.19 (was 0.35), never under 0.1. Let go: he still snaps back with the wobble, then he **pops**: he swells and fades in 0.2 s with a 💥 burst where he was (a `div` on the page for half a second). **He no longer flies off the screen spinning.** He still stays away 10 minutes on that person's screen. Where the notes above say he flies off, read this. Checked in a test browser (thickness, gone after the pop). **Not seen on the live app.**
  - **The pictures** (`ECO_LIB`, files in `eco/…​.webp`): the five still faces, plus what he dragged in at 8:34 p.m.: `eco-3d-pointing` (still), and four that move with no background: `eco-3d-pointing-move`, `eco-3d-talking-move`, `eco-3d-walk-move`, `eco-3d-impatient-move` (made smaller, 320 to 360px high, half the frames, 0.5 to 1 MB each; they are only downloaded when shown). **To add a picture: put the .webp in `eco/` and add a line to `ECO_LIB`.** **13 more that move were added Nov 10, 10:10 p.m.** (Carlo, from the chat where the 3D Eco Man is made: "add these to the eco man page as well… move the other ones you just made there as well… make sure all 3d images you made here are in"): the four faces moving (`eco-3d-happy-move` flexing, `-confused-move` shrugging, `-sad-move` crying, `-angry-move` shaking his fists; made from the see-through frames, so no pale blue background), four dances (`eco-3d-dance-turn-shake-move`, `-side-shuffle-`, `-spin-`, `-disco-`), and `eco-3d-jump-move`, `-front-flip-`, `-back-flip-`, `-cartwheel-`, `eco-3d-run-jump-cartwheel-move` (wide: he runs in from the left and cartwheels out on the right). Then at 10:57 p.m. ("a standing back flip, a standing front flip, have him explode, have him shing then pop and disappear; remember add all of them to the page"): `eco-3d-standing-back-flip-move`, `eco-3d-standing-front-flip-move`, `eco-3d-explode-move` (he shakes, swells, bursts into pieces, then pops back whole) and `eco-3d-shine-pop-disappear-move` (I took "shing" as shine: he pops in, stars twinkle on him, he pops and is gone, and the last 0.9 s of the picture is empty before it starts again).   - **The live Eco Man: 🕹 Play with Eco Man, at the top of Settings → Eco Man** (Carlo, Nov 10, 11:12 p.m.: "make one that I can interact with, I can poke him, push him and have him react in different ways", then 11:31 p.m.: "load him to the page"). A button, **▶ Open the live Eco Man**, shows him in a frame on the tab (`ECO_SET.play`, `data-eco-play`, `iframe.eco-play`); **✕ Put him away** closes it. He is a real 3D model drawn live, not a picture: **his own page, `eco/poke.html`**, which loads three.js (0.160.0, from jsdelivr, the same place the app gets Supabase) only when the button is pressed, so the app itself did not get heavier. What he does: poke his eyes, door, mouth, roof, chimney, arms or shoes (each has its own reaction and lines); hold his roof and pull sideways and he leans and wobbles back; pull far and he falls over, sees stars and gets up; pull his roof down and let go and he springs up; flick him up fast and he jumps (faster: a flip); flick him sideways fast and he spins and gets dizzy; hold his shoes and drag to slide him; poke him 6 times in 4 seconds and he gets angry, 11 and he flips away. His eyes follow the mouse; left alone 9 seconds he asks to be poked. The page lists these and ticks each off, and counts pokes and knock-overs (nothing is saved, the boss is not told). Space or Enter pokes him, the arrow keys push and jump. **He is only in this frame: he can not be put on other pages with ➕ Add him, and he is not in `ECO_LIB`.** To change him, edit `eco/poke.html` (one file: the model, the looks in `LOOK`, the reactions in `REACT`, the rules in `drop` and `frame`). The tab is drawn again when a place or picture is clicked, which starts him over. Owners only, like the tab. Checked: the app's script parses; his page was run in a test browser and each move was made to happen (the test browser is slow, so how it feels at full speed was not seen). **Not seen on the live app, and not tried with a finger on a phone.**
  - **The live Eco Man can stand anywhere on a page, with no frame** (Carlo, Nov 10, 11:34 p.m.: "can he live out of the frame and be placeable anywhere on the app?"; I told him what changes; 11:36 p.m.: "build him even if some things don't work on him… and load him in the page"). In **➕ Add him to this page** the picture box has one more choice, the last one: **LIVE: you can poke and push him** (`eco-3d-live` in `ECO_LIB`). Put on a page, he is the real 3D model, see-through around him, and everyone can poke him, push him over, squash him, throw him, spin him and slide him, right on the page. The size buttons and the slider work on him.
    - How: `ecoSpotsApply` draws the spot as always (the `img`, here the still picture `eco/eco-3d-live.webp`), then `ecoLiveMount(sp, s)` loads three.js (0.160.0, jsdelivr) and `eco/live.js` (`ecoLiveLoad`, once) and calls `EcoLive.mount(sp, { px, frozen })`. That puts a drawing area over the spot (3.2 times his height wide, 2.3 times high, so he has room to fall and jump; `.eco-live-box`, clicks go through it) and a small box that follows him and takes the clicks (`.eco-live-hit`), so buttons beside him still work. The picture is hidden once he is drawn (`.eco-spot.live-on`). He stops drawing when he is off the screen or the tab is hidden, and cleans up when the page is drawn again (then he is made again and starts over).
    - **With the 🏠 Eco Man bar open** the live one is put away and his still picture shows with the violet frame, so a click on him opens the box to change his size, change him to a picture, or take him off, like any other.
    - **At most two live ones on a page** (I chose it; a third shows as his still picture): each is a 3D drawing that keeps running.
